    26	#[derive(serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
    27	struct Evidence { #[serde(default)] absent:BTreeSet<String>, #[serde(default)] epoch:i64, active:bool, inputs: Value, before: BTreeMap<String,Value>, after: BTreeMap<String,Value> }
    28	fn evidence(tx: &Tx<'_>, t:i64,id:&str,key:&str) -> Result<Option<Evidence>,RuntimeError> {
    29	    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(inputs)+octet_length(before_image)+octet_length(after_image) FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3",params![t,id,key],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
    30	    let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap(bytes.unwrap_or(0).max(0) as u64 * 64 + 8192)?;
    31	    let value:Option<(String,String,String,bool,i64)>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT inputs,before_image,after_image,active,epoch FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3",params![t,id,key],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?,r.get(4)?))).optional()?;
    32	    value.map(|(inputs,before,after,active,epoch)|Ok(Evidence {absent:absence(tx,t,id,key)?,epoch,active,inputs:decode(&inputs)?,before:decode(&before)?,after:decode(&after)?})).transpose()
    33	}
    34	fn decode<T:serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(s:&str)->Result<T,RuntimeError>{serde_json::from_str(s).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))}
    35	fn absence(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,key:&str)->Result<BTreeSet<String>,RuntimeError>{
    36	    Ok(tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3")?.query_map(params![t,id,key],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?)
    37	}
    38	fn save(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,key:&str,e:&Evidence,active:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    39	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _backfill_evidence(t,id,definition,inputs,before_image,after_image,active,epoch) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7,?8) ON CONFLICT(t,id,definition) DO UPDATE SET inputs=excluded.inputs,before_image=excluded.before_image,after_image=excluded.after_image,active=excluded.active,epoch=excluded.epoch",params![t,id,key,canonical_json(&e.inputs),canonical_json(&e.before),canonical_json(&e.after),active,e.epoch])?;
    40	    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3",params![t,id,key])?;
    41	    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3",params![t,id,key])?;
    42	    for c in &e.absent {tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _backfill_absence VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",params![t,id,key,c])?;}
    43	    Ok(())
    44	}
    45	pub(super) fn delete(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    46	    if present(tx)? {
    47	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
    48	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
    49	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
    50	    } Ok(())
    51	}
    52	// Never archive reference/asset preimages without lifetime evidence. New reference
    53	// fields are safe: subsequent retirement is owned by the ordinary dormant ledger.
    54	fn protected(kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType)->bool {
    55	    use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;
    56	    match kind {Optional(k)=>protected(k),Ref{..}|Image{..}|Video{..}|File{..}|List{..}|ListRange{..}=>true,_=>false}
    57	}
    58	pub(super) fn transition(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,now:i64,report:&mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    59	    if next.backfills.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s|s.backfills.is_empty()) && !present(tx)? { return Ok(()); }
    60	    enable(tx)?;
    61	    for fill in &next.backfills { fill.admit(next).map_err(refusal)?; }
    62	    // A name is immutable within a continuous lineage. Replacing a transform
    63	    // deliberately goes through an old-source/bridge transition first.
    64	    if let Some(old)=previous {for f in &next.backfills {if old.backfills.iter().any(|p|p.table==f.table && p.name==f.name && p.key()!=f.key()) {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {} changed; use a new name in a bridge release rather than replacing historical meaning",f.table,f.name)));}}}
    65	    for fill in &next.backfills {
    66	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_definitions VALUES(?1,?2)",params![fill.key(),canonical_json(fill)])?;
    67	    }
    68	    let next_keys:BTreeSet<_>=next.backfills.iter().map(Backfill::key).collect();
    69	    let definitions:Vec<Backfill>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE active=1) ORDER BY digest")?.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.map(|s|decode(&s?)).collect::<Result<_,RuntimeError>>()?;
    70	    for fill in definitions.iter().rev().filter(|f|!next_keys.contains(&f.key())) {
    71	        let Some(table)=next.table(&fill.table) else {continue};
    72	        let t=tx.num(&fill.table)?;let key=fill.key();let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();let mut count=0;
    73	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
    74	            let Some(e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&key)? else {
    75	                let pruned:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3 AND active=1)",params![t,id,key],|r|r.get(0))?;
    76	                if pruned && fill.outputs.iter().any(|c|table.columns.contains_key(c)) {bad.add(id,"rollback preimage was explicitly pruned; select a bridge preserving current data or restore a backup");}
    77	                return Ok(())
    78	            };
    79	            if !e.active {return Ok(())}
    80	            let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid stored row"))};
    81	            let mut changed=false;
    82	            for c in &fill.outputs {if table.columns.contains_key(c) {
    83	                let current=fields.get(c);let before=e.before.get(c);let after=e.after.get(c);
    84	                if current!=after && current!=before {bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} changed after backfill {}; keep a bridge and repair through an authorized mutation before rollback",fill.table,c,fill.name));return Ok(())}
    85	                if let Some(value)=before {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{fields.remove(c);}
    86	                changed=true;
    87	            }}
    88	            if changed {tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;count+=1;}
    89	            save(tx,t,id,&key,&e,false)
    90	        })?;
    91	        if bad.count>0 {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {} rollback: {}",fill.table,fill.name,bad.describe())))}
    92	        if count>0 {report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} reversed on {count} {}",fill.table,fill.name,if count==1 {"row"}else{"rows"}));tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;}
    93	    }
    94	    let old_keys:BTreeSet<_>=previous.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.backfills.iter().map(Backfill::key)).collect();
    95	    let restored_tables:BTreeSet<i64>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT n.t FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)")?.query_map([],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    96	    let entering:Vec<_>=next.backfills.iter().filter(|f|!old_keys.contains(&f.key()) || tx.num(&f.table).is_ok_and(|t|restored_tables.contains(&t))).collect();
    97	    if entering.is_empty(){return Ok(())}
    98	    let populated = entering.iter().try_fold(false, |any,fill| -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    99	        Ok(any || tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1)",[tx.num(&fill.table)?],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?)
   100	    })?;
   101	    if !populated {return Ok(())}
   102	    let mut source=previous.cloned().unwrap_or_default();source.tables=sources.clone();source.backfills.clear();
   103	    // Point lookup indexes are rebuilt against the assembled source images,
   104	    // including restored rows, before any output patch is applied.
   105	    let mut reads=BTreeSet::new();
   106	    for f in &entering {collect_reads(&f.program.body,&mut reads);}
   107	    for (name,index) in &reads {
   108	        let wanted=next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.index(&index)).ok_or_else(||refusal("backfill lookup index absent"))?;
   109	        let old_table=source.tables.get_mut(name).ok_or_else(||refusal(format!("backfill lookup source {name} has no historical definition")))?;
   110	        if !wanted.is_plain() || wanted.columns().iter().any(|c|c!="id" && !old_table.columns.contains_key(c)) {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill lookup {name}.{index} requires predecessor fields; use an indexed bridge")))}
   111	        old_table.indexes.insert(index.clone(),wanted.clone());
   112	    }
   113	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TEMP TABLE _backfill_pending(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,patch TEXT NOT NULL,evidence TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,definition)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   114	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TEMP TABLE _backfill_originals(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   115	    let entering_keys:BTreeSet<_>=entering.iter().map(|f|f.key()).collect();
   116	    let mut epochs=BTreeSet::from([None]);
   117	    for key in &entering_keys {
   118	        let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT epoch FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE definition=?1 AND active=1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id)")?;
   119	        for epoch in q.query_map([key],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0))? {epochs.insert(Some(epoch?));}
   120	    }
   121	    let fresh_epoch=tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0).saturating_add(1);
   122	    for epoch in epochs {
   123	        restore_snapshot(tx)?;
   124	        if let Some(epoch)=epoch { historical_snapshot(tx,epoch,&next_keys)?; } else { inactive_snapshot(tx,previous,&entering_keys)?; }
   125	        rebuild_reads(tx,&source,&reads)?;
   126	    for fill in &entering {
   127	
   128	        let t=tx.num(&fill.table)?;let key=fill.key();let table=&next.tables[&fill.table];let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();let mut count=0;
   129	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
   130	            if old_keys.contains(&key) && !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {return Ok(())}
   131	            let prior=evidence(tx,t,id,&key)?;
   132	            if prior.as_ref().filter(|e|e.active).map(|e|e.epoch)!=epoch {return Ok(())}
   133	            let original:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM temp._backfill_originals WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   134	            let current:Value=if let Some(text)=original {decode(&text)?}else{row.clone()};
   135	            if prior.is_none() && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3)",params![t,id,key],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   136	                bad.add(id,"historical backfill evidence was explicitly pruned; cannot safely re-enter this declaration; use a new bridge or restore a backup");return Ok(())
   137	            }
   138	            for c in fill.inputs.keys() {
   139	                if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   140	                    bad.add(id,&format!("historical reference {}.{c} lost its parent lifetime",fill.table));return Ok(())
   141	                }
   142	            }
   143	            let physically_absent=absence(tx,t,id,"")?;
   144	            let native=absence(tx,t,id,&format!("$native:{key}"))?;
   145	            if prior.is_none() && !native.is_empty() {
   146	                let native_inputs=native_inputs(tx,t,id,&current,fill.inputs.keys().filter(|c|!fill.outputs.contains(*c)).map(String::as_str),sources.get(&fill.table))?;
   147	                if fill.inputs.iter().any(|(c,kind)|!fill.outputs.contains(c) && !physically_absent.contains(c) && crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,kind,native_inputs.get(c)).is_err()) {
   148	                    bad.add(id,"native image has an invalid present historical input; use a typed bridge");return Ok(())
   149	                }
   150	                let mut conflict=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?;
   151	                let others:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   152	                for other in others {if let Some(e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&other)? {
   153	                    for c in fill.outputs.intersection(&e.after.keys().cloned().collect()) {
   154	                        if e.active || current.field(c).ok()!=e.before.get(c) {conflict=true;}
   155	                    }
   156	                }}
   157	                if conflict {
   158	                    bad.add(id,"native output also has a different transformation's history; resolve its intended view in a bridge");return Ok(())
   159	                }
   160	                let complete=fill.outputs.iter().all(|c|current.field(c).is_ok() && crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,&table.columns[c],current.field(c).ok()).is_ok());
   161	                let fingerprint=crate::value::digest(&native_inputs);
   162	                if !complete {bad.add(id,"native backfill output image is missing or was explicitly pruned; use a bridge or restore a backup");return Ok(())}
   163	                if !native.contains(&fingerprint) {bad.add(id,"native backfill inputs changed after row creation; preserve the authored output in a bridge before re-entry");return Ok(())}
   164	                return Ok(())
   165	            }
   166	            let original_absence=prior.as_ref().filter(|e|e.active).map_or_else(||physically_absent.intersection(&fill.inputs.keys().cloned().collect()).cloned().collect(),|e|e.absent.clone());
   167	            // Resolve retained inputs before source defaults can synthesize null
   168	            // for a hidden optional column with a provided archived cell.
   169	            let retained=native_inputs(tx,t,id,&row,fill.inputs.keys().map(String::as_str),sources.get(&fill.table))?;
   170	            if let Some(table)=sources.get(&fill.table){materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);}
   171	            let historical=row.clone();
   172	            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
   173	            for (c,kind) in &fill.inputs {
   174	                let value=retained.get(c).cloned();
   175	                if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,kind,value.as_ref()).is_err() {
   176	                    bad.add(id,&format!("historical input {}.{c} is absent or does not fit {kind}; use a typed bridge to repair it",fill.table));return Ok(())
   177	                }
   178	                args.insert(c.clone(),value.unwrap_or(Value::Null));
   179	            }
   180	            let inputs=Value::Row(args.clone().into());
   181	            let mut context=Context::system().at(now);context.args=args;
   182	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,context,Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),0);
   183	            let patch=match runner.run(&fill.program) {Ok(Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f))=>f.into_iter().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),Ok(_)=>{bad.add(id,"backfill did not return an object");return Ok(())},Err(e)=>{bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}};
   184	            if runner.read_cut().is_some() {bad.add(id,"historical lookup did not complete; reduce per-row lookup work before adoption");return Ok(())}
   185	            if patch.keys().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()!=fill.outputs {return Err(refusal("backfill returned undeclared output fields"))}
   186	            let fields=current.as_object()?;let mut before=prior.as_ref().filter(|e|e.active).map_or_else(BTreeMap::new,|e|e.before.clone());let mut desired=patch.clone();
   187	            for (c,value) in &patch {
   188	                if let Err(e)=crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,&table.columns[c],Some(value)) {bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}
   189	                if let Some(kind)=sources.get(&fill.table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)) {if protected(kind) && prior.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.before.contains_key(c)) {bad.add(id,&format!("cannot overwrite historical reference or asset {}.{c}; derive into a new column",fill.table));return Ok(())}}
   190	                if !prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.active) && (previous.and_then(|s|s.table(&fill.table)).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)) || (previous.and_then(|s|s.table(&fill.table)).is_none() && sources.get(&fill.table).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)))) {
   191	                    if let Some(v)=historical.field(c).ok(){before.insert(c.clone(),v.clone());}
   192	                }
   193	                if let Some(prior)=&prior {
   194	                    let deps_same=prior.inputs==inputs && prior.after==patch;
   195	                    let current=fields.get(c);
   196	                    let original=prior.before.get(c);
   197	                    if deps_same && (prior.active || original.is_none()) && current.is_some() {
   198	                        // Added columns retain edits under v1's dormant storage.
   199	                        desired.insert(c.clone(),current.unwrap().clone());
   200	                    } else if !deps_same && current!=prior.after.get(c) && current!=original {
   201	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} was edited and its historical inputs changed; keep a bridge and resolve the conflict",fill.table,c));return Ok(())
   202	                    }
   203	                }
   204	            }
   205	            let e=Evidence{absent:original_absence,epoch:epoch.unwrap_or(fresh_epoch),active:true,inputs,before,after:patch};
   206	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO temp._backfill_pending VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",params![t,id,key,canonical_json(&desired),canonical_json(&e)])?;count+=1;
   207	            Ok(())
   208	        })?;
   209	        if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {}: {}",fill.table,fill.name,bad.describe())))}
   210	        if count>0{report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} transformed {count} {}",fill.table,fill.name,if count==1 {"row"}else{"rows"}));}
   211	    }
   212	    }
   213	    restore_snapshot(tx)?;
   214	    // Stream patches; avoid retaining the whole transformed population in RAM.
   215	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition,patch,evidence FROM temp._backfill_pending ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   216	    let mut rows=stmt.query([])?;
   217	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   218	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;
   219	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;let e:Evidence=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(4)?)?;
   220	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   221	        if let Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=&mut row {f.extend(patch);}
   222	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   223	        save(tx,t,&id,&key,&e,true)?;
   224	        for c in e.after.keys() {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition='' AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c])?;}
   225	        let fill=next.backfills.iter().find(|f|f.key()==key).ok_or_else(||refusal("missing historical declaration"))?;
   226	        for(c,kind)in &fill.inputs {
   227	            // A generic id argument cannot erase the source column's lifetime.
   228	            let kind=sources.get(&fill.table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)).filter(|k|protected(k)).unwrap_or(kind);
   229	            if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c) {super::dormant::capture_refs(tx,t,&id,&input_ref(&key,c),kind,value)?;}
   230	        }
   231	    }
   232	    drop(rows);drop(stmt);
   233	    tx.conn().execute_batch("DROP TABLE temp._backfill_pending; DROP TABLE temp._backfill_originals;")?;
   234	    // The source-index rebuild and every transformed dependency require full
   235	    // candidate key/cap/maintain validation, even when its declaration is equal.
   236	    tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;
   237	    Ok(())
   238	}
   239	fn collect_reads(body:&[crate::ir::Stmt],out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){
   240	    use crate::ir::{Expr,Stmt};
   241	    fn expr(e:&Expr,out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){match e{
   242	        Expr::Scan(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for k in &r.prefix{if let Some(e)=k.scalar(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(range)=&r.range {for e in [&range.gt,&range.gte,&range.lt,&range.lte].into_iter().flatten(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(e)=&r.filter{expr(e,out);}},
   243	        Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for e in &r.key{expr(e,out);}},
   244	        Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base,out),Expr::Array(es)|Expr::Builtin{args:es,..}=>{for e in es{expr(e,out);}},
   245	        Expr::Object(fs)=>{for e in fs.values(){expr(e,out);}},Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>{expr(base,out);for e in fields.values(){expr(e,out);}},
   246	        Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>{expr(left,out);expr(right,out);},Expr::Block{body,value}=>{collect_reads(body,out);expr(value,out);},_=>()
   247	    }}
   248	    for s in body{match s{Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value,out),Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>expr(condition,out),Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>{expr(condition,out);collect_reads(then_body,out);collect_reads(else_body,out);},_=>()}}
   249	}
   250	
   251	// Prune forgets historical values, retaining only the fact that exact reversal
   252	// is no longer available. No tombstone can be queried by an app.
   253	pub(super) fn prune(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   254	    if present(tx)? {tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id); DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_pruned.t AND r.id=_backfill_pruned.id); INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_pruned SELECT t,id,definition,active FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE (definition!='' AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%') OR NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_absence.t AND r.id=_backfill_absence.id);")?;}
   255	    Ok(())
   256	}
   257	pub(super) fn inventory_sql(conn:&Connection)->Result<&'static str,RuntimeError>{
   258	    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_backfill_evidence')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
   259	    Ok(if exists {" UNION ALL SELECT c.name||'.backfill#'||substr(e.definition,1,12),'backfill',c.name,e.definition FROM _backfill_evidence e JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=e.t GROUP BY e.t,e.definition "}else{""})
   260	}
   261	
   262	pub(crate) fn has_evidence(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{present(tx)}
   263	pub(super) fn columns(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema)->Result<BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>,RuntimeError>{
   264	    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
   265	    for f in &next.backfills {out.entry(f.table.clone()).or_default().extend(f.outputs.iter().cloned());}
   266	    if present(tx)? {
   267	        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE active=1)")?;
   268	        for text in statement.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {
   269	            let f:Backfill=decode(&text?)?;out.entry(f.table).or_default().extend(f.outputs);
   270	        }
   271	    }
   272	    Ok(out)
   273	}
   274	fn validate_lookup(tx:&Tx<'_>,ti:i64,prefix:usize,table:&str,index:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   275	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k,id FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=stmt.query([ti])?;
   276	    let mut prior=None;let mut first=String::new();let mut duplicate=false;let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   277	    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   278	        let key:Vec<u8>=row.get(0)?;let id:String=row.get(1)?;let mut group=crate::key::decode(&key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical lookup key"))?;group.truncate(prefix);
   279	        if group.contains(&Value::Null){continue;}
   280	        if prior.as_ref()==Some(&group) {
   281	            if !duplicate {bad.add(&first,"duplicate historical lookup key");duplicate=true;}
   282	            bad.add(&id,"duplicate historical lookup key");
   283	        }else{prior=Some(group);first=id;duplicate=false;}
   284	    }
   285	    if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("historical lookup {table}.{index} is not unique: {}; prepare a unique bridge before deriving values",bad.describe())))}
   286	    Ok(())
   287	}
   288	
   289	// Each original adoption cohort saw one snapshot. Re-entry restores that same
   290	// view across all its declarations; later cohorts read earlier cohorts' outputs.
   291	fn restore_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   292	    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=(SELECT o.data FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id)",[])?;Ok(())
   293	}
   294	fn historical_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   295	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   296	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   297	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   298	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   299	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   300	        let Some(data)=data else {continue};
   301	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   302	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   303	        let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid historical row"))};
   304	        if let Value::Row(inputs)|Value::Map(inputs)=e.inputs {for(c,value)in inputs {
   305	            if e.absent.contains(&c) || !fields.contains_key(&c) || fields.get(&c)==Some(&value) {
   306	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,&c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   307	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   308	                if e.absent.contains(&c) {fields.insert(c,value);}else{fields.entry(c).or_insert(value);}
   309	            }
   310	        }}
   311	        for c in e.after.keys() {if !e.before.contains_key(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}}
   312	        for(c,value)in e.before {fields.insert(c,value);}
   313	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   314	    } Ok(())
   315	}
   316	fn rebuild_reads(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,reads:&BTreeSet<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   317	    for (name,index) in reads {
   318	        let old=&source.tables[name].indexes[index];let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;
   319	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   320	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,mut row|{
   321	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   322	            for key in index_keys(old,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   323	        })?;
   324	        if old.unique {validate_lookup(tx,ti,old.components.len(),name,index)?;}
   325	    } Ok(())
   326	}

   328	fn input_ref(key:&str,column:&str)->String {format!("$backfill:{key}:{column}")}
   329	
   330	// A prior transformation's retained added output is not its own old argument
   331	// on forward selection after rollback. Other declarations still see the new
   332	// predecessor snapshot rather than an old cohort's overwritten columns.
   333	fn inactive_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,previous:Option<&Schema>,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   334	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=0 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   335	    let mut rows=q.query([])?;
   336	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   337	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   338	        let name:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=?1",[t],|r|r.get(0))?;
   339	        let old=previous.and_then(|s|s.table(&name));
   340	        let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   341	        let absent:Vec<_>=e.after.keys().filter(|c|!e.before.contains_key(*c) && old.is_none_or(|t|!t.columns.contains_key(*c))).cloned().collect();
   342	        if absent.is_empty(){continue;}
   343	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;let Some(data)=data else{continue};
   344	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   345	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {for c in absent {fields.insert(c,Value::Null);}}
   346	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   347	    }Ok(())
   348	}
   349	
   350	// Empty definition denotes currently omitted physical input cells; a definition
   351	// digest denotes absence at that receipt's adoption. Defaults may materialize
   352	// during retention without turning omitted inputs into authored values.
   353	pub(crate) fn prepare_inputs(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   354	    if next.backfills.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s|s.backfills.is_empty()) && !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   355	    enable(tx)?;
   356	    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_active",[])?;
   357	    for fill in &next.backfills {tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _backfill_active VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![tx.num(&fill.table)?,fill.key(),canonical_json(&fill.inputs.keys().filter(|c|!fill.outputs.contains(*c)).collect::<Vec<_>>())])?;}
   358	    for fill in next.backfills.iter().chain(previous.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.backfills.iter())) {
   359	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_definitions VALUES(?1,?2)",params![fill.key(),canonical_json(fill)])?;
   360	        for c in fill.inputs.keys() {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_inputs VALUES(?1,?2)",params![tx.num(&fill.table)?,c])?;}
   361	    }
   362	    let tables:Vec<i64>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT t FROM _backfill_inputs")?.query_map([],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   363	    for t in tables {
   364	        let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   365	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,row|{
   366	            for c in &columns {if row.field(c).is_err(){note_omission(tx,t,id,c)?;}}
   367	            Ok(())
   368	        })?;
   369	    } Ok(())
   370	}
   371	pub(super) fn observe_write(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   372	    if !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   373	    if old.is_none() {
   374	        let active:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition,input_names FROM _backfill_active WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   375	        for (key,names) in active {
   376	            let names:Vec<String>=decode(&names)?;
   377	            let fingerprint=input_fingerprint(&crate::schema::row_image(row,table),names.iter().map(String::as_str));
   378	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_absence VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",params![t,id,format!("$native:{key}"),fingerprint])?;
   379	        }
   380	    }
   381	    let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   382	    if columns.is_empty(){return Ok(());}
   383	    let interpreted_old=old.map(|r|crate::schema::row_image(r,table));
   384	    let interpreted_new=crate::schema::row_image(row,table);
   385	    for c in columns {
   386	        let changed=interpreted_old.as_ref().is_none_or(|before|before.field(&c).ok()!=interpreted_new.field(&c).ok());
   387	        if changed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%'",params![t,id,c])?;}
   388	        if row.field(&c).is_err() {note_omission(tx,t,id,&c)?;}
   389	    } Ok(())
   390	}
   391	
   392	pub(super) fn record_omissions(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   393	    if !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   394	    let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   395	    for c in columns {if row.field(&c).is_err(){note_omission(tx,t,id,&c)?;}}Ok(())
   396	}
   397	
   398	fn input_fingerprint<'a>(row:&Value,names:impl Iterator<Item=&'a str>)->String {
   399	    let fields:BTreeMap<String,Value>=names.map(|c|(c.to_owned(),row.field(c).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null))).collect();
   400	    crate::value::digest(&fields)
   401	}
   402	
   403	fn note_omission(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,c:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   404	    // A provided historical cell stays provided when merely moved out of the
   405	    // active projection. Already-recorded omission survives default freezing.
   406	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_absence SELECT ?1,?2,'',?3 WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,c])?;Ok(())
   407	}
   408	fn native_inputs<'a>(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value,names:impl Iterator<Item=&'a str>,table:Option<&Table>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,RuntimeError>{
   409	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   410	    for c in names {
   411	        let value=if let Ok(value)=row.field(c){meter.heap(value.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;value.clone()}else{
   412	            let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   413	            meter.heap(bytes.unwrap_or(0).max(0) as u64 * 64 + 8192)?;
   414	            let text:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   415	            text.map(|s|decode(&s)).transpose()?.or_else(||table.and_then(|t|t.defaults.get(c)).and_then(|d|if let crate::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(v)=d {Some(v.clone())}else{None})).unwrap_or(Value::Null)
   416	        };out.insert(c.to_owned(),value);
   417	    }Ok(out)
   418	}
   419	
   420	// @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — source expiry is an ordinary reference-action closure, never an unchecked physical delete.
   421	pub(super) fn retire_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   422	    let mut source=previous.cloned().unwrap_or_default();source.tables=sources.clone();source.backfills.clear();source.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
   423	    let names:Vec<String>=source.through_order().into_iter().rev().filter(|n|source.tables[n].ephemeral.is_none() && source.maintain_for(n).is_none()).collect();
   424	    let mut due=false;
   425	    for name in &names {
   426	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   427	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,row|{if !due {due=source_expired(tx,t,id,&source.tables[name],&row,now)?;}Ok(())})?;
   428	    }
   429	    if !due{return Ok(())}
   430	    // Restored sources have no live index entries. Every reference action,
   431	    // maintained dependency and retained-child probe uses this assembled view.
   432	    for name in &names {
   433	        let table=&source.tables[name];
   434	        for (index,definition) in &table.indexes {
   435	            let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   436	            let work=crate::rows::adoption::Work::table(table);
   437	            let scratch=|row:&Value|crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(row,table);
   438	            super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,tx.num(name)?,work,if work.index_entries!=0 {Some(&scratch)}else{None},|id,mut row,_|{
   439	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);
   440	                for key in index_keys(definition,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   441	            })?;
   442	        }
   443	    }
   444	    if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_cap_counts')",[],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   445	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _cap_counts",[])?;
   446	        for name in &names {let table=&source.tables[name];super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|_,row|tx.update_cap_counts(name,table,None,Some(&row)).map(|_|()))?;}
   447	    }
   448	    validate_action_bounds(tx,&source,now)?;
   449	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
   450	    for name in &names {
   451	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   452	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,_|{
   453	            let Some(mut row)=tx.get(name,id)? else{return Ok(())};
   454	            if !source_expired(tx,t,id,&source.tables[name],&row,now)? {return Ok(())}
   455	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   456	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,Context::system().at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),tx.next_seq()?);
   457	            match runner.retire_expired_row(name,&row) {
   458	                Ok(true)=>{tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;},
   459	                Ok(false)=>return Err(refusal(format!("source expiry {name}/{id} is blocked by retained children; resolve them in a bridge before adoption"))),
   460	                Err(e)=>return Err(refusal(format!("source expiry {name}/{id}: {e}"))),
   461	            }Ok(())
   462	        })?;
   463	    }
   464	    for name in &names {
   465	        let cleared:Vec<_>=source.tables[name].on_delete.iter().filter(|(_,a)|**a==crate::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull).map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect();
   466	        if cleared.is_empty(){continue;}
   467	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,row|{
   468	            for c in &cleared {if row.field(c).is_ok_and(|v|*v==Value::Null){tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![tx.num(name)?,id,c])?;}}Ok(())
   469	        })?;
   470	    }
   471	    tx.clear_activation_scan();Ok(())
   472	}
   473	fn source_expired(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
   474	    if table.expired(row,now){return Ok(true)}
   475	    let definition:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   476	    if let Some(definition)=definition {let old:Table=decode(&definition)?;return Ok(old.expired(row,now));}Ok(false)
   477	}
   478	
   479	fn validate_action_bounds(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   480	    for parent in source.tables.keys() {for r in source.referencing(parent).into_iter().filter(|r|r.action.writes()) {
   481	        let bound=source.reference_cap(r.child,r.column).ok_or_else(||refusal("historical reference action has no bound"))?;
   482	        let index=source.reference_index(r.child,r.column).ok_or_else(||refusal("historical reference index absent"))?;
   483	        let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=q.query([tx.num(&format!("{}.{index}",r.child))?])?;
   484	        let mut prior=None;let mut count=0u64;let mut visited=0u64;let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   485	        while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   486	            if visited%256==0 {meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);}visited+=1;
   487	            let mark=meter.mark();let key=row.get_ref(0)?.as_blob().map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;meter.heap(key.len() as u64 * 64 + 8192)?;
   488	            let values=crate::key::decode(key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical reference key"))?;
   489	            if values.first().is_none_or(|v|*v==Value::Null) || r.table.is_expiring() && values.get(1).is_some_and(|v|matches!(v,Value::Int(at) if *at<=now)) {meter.release_heap(mark);continue;}
   490	            if prior.as_ref()==values.first(){count+=1;}else{prior=values.first().cloned();count=1;}
   491	            if count>u64::from(bound){return Err(refusal(format!("historical reference group {}.{} exceeds retirement bound {bound}; reconcile retained capacity in a bridge before adoption",r.child,r.column)));}
   492	            meter.release_heap(mark);
   493	        }
   494	    }}Ok(())
   495	}

                if !plan.project && plan.indexes.is_empty() { continue; }
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                for index in &plan.indexes { tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1", [catalog[&format!("{name}.{index}")]])?; }
                let work = crate::rows::adoption::Work::table(table);
                let scratch = |row: &Value| crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(row, table);
                walk_metered(&tx, t, work, if work.index_entries != 0 { Some(&scratch) } else { None }, |id, mut row, contribution_meter| {
                    let keep = |column: &str| {
                            table.columns.contains_key(column) || (programs.is_none() && column == "pending")
                        };
                    let project = plan.project && row.as_object().is_ok_and(|fields| fields.keys().any(|column| !keep(column)));
                    // Persist only a destructive projection. Defaults and
                    // optionals are an interpretation of the stored image.
                    if project {
                        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.retain(|column, _| keep(column)); }
                        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = ?1 WHERE t = ?2 AND id = ?3", params![canonical_json(&row), t, id])?;
                    }
                    materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                    // A device's prediction mark is metadata, retained in
                    // storage but absent from the row the schema validates.
                    if programs.is_none() { if let Value::Row(fields) = &mut row { fields.remove("pending"); } }
                    // Maintained rows are replaced by the candidate builder.
                    if schema.maintain_for(name).is_none() { crate::engine::validate::validate_row(schema, name, &row)?; }
                    for index_name in &plan.indexes {
                        let index = &table.indexes[index_name];
                        let scratch = contribution_meter.mark();
                        let contribution = match index.components.first() { Some(crate::schema::IndexComponent::Contribution(m)) => Some(m), _ => None };
                        if let Some(m) = contribution {
                            contribution_meter.at(&format!("adopt {name}.{index_name}"));
                            contribution_meter.charge(crate::error::MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
                            contribution_meter.step(m.evaluation_steps())?;
                            crate::rows::adoption::charge(contribution_meter, crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(&row, table))?;
                        }
                        for key in index_keys(index, &row, id) {
                            if key.len() > crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES {
                                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(name, index_name, 1));
                            }
                            if contribution.is_some() { contribution_meter.charge(crate::error::MeterKind::IndexEntries, 1)?; }
                            super::activation_index::insert(&tx, ordered, catalog[&format!("{name}.{index_name}")], &key, id)?;
//! Reuse physical state whose complete interpretation has not changed.
use crate::schema::Schema;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

pub(super) struct Plan {
    pub project: bool,
    pub indexes: BTreeSet<String>,
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — an added view must not reconstruct unrelated rows and indexes.
// Compare the whole table except indexes. This intentionally keeps the old
// exhaustive validation for defaults, captures, rules, lifetimes and constraints.
// An index can survive an unrelated column change only when each interpreted
// dependency (including calendar, words and contribution predicates) is equal.
// Device installations have no persisted server predecessor and keep that path.
pub(super) fn plans(next: &Schema, previous: Option<&Schema>) -> BTreeMap<String, Plan> {
    next.tables
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, table)| {
            let old = previous.and_then(|schema| schema.table(name));
            let project = old.is_none_or(|old| {
                let mut comparable = table.clone();
                comparable.indexes = old.indexes.clone();
                &comparable != old
            });
            let indexes = table
                .indexes
                .iter()
                .filter(|(index_name, index)| {
                    old.is_none_or(|old| {
                        old.indexes.get(*index_name) != Some(*index)
                            || old.through != table.through
                            || old.columns.keys().chain(table.columns.keys()).any(|field| {
                                index.uses_column(field)
                                    && (crate::successor::interpretation(
                                        previous.unwrap(),
                                        name,
                                        field,
                                    ) != crate::successor::interpretation(next, name, field)
                                        || old.maintained.get(field) != table.maintained.get(field))
                            })
                    })
                })
                .map(|(name, _)| name.clone())
                .collect();
            (name.clone(), Plan { project, indexes })
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Historical catalog numbers remain, but inactive names lose their rows/keys.
pub(super) fn active_names(schema: &Schema) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none())
        .flat_map(|(name, table)| std::iter::once(name.clone())
            .chain(table.indexes.keys().map(move |index| format!("{name}.{index}"))))
        .collect()
}
